Revision ID: 20260916_m2_c_active_private_chat

"""

from collections.abc import Sequence

from alembic import op
import sqlalchemy as sa
from sqlalchemy.dialects import postgresql


revision: str = "20260916_m2_c_active_private_chat"

down_revision: str | Sequence[str] | None = "20260915_m2_c_private_chat"

branch_labels: str | Sequence[str] | None = None

depends_on: str | Sequence[str] | None = None


def upgrade() -> None:
    """Create the server-owned active conversation pointer."""

    op.create_table(
        "private_chat_active_conversations",
        sa.Column(
            "user_id",
            sa.String(length=255),
            nullable=False,
        ),
        sa.Column(
            "world_id",
            sa.String(length=255),
            nullable=False,
        ),
        sa.Column(
            "conversation_id",
            postgresql.UUID(as_uuid=True),
            nullable=False,
        ),
        sa.ForeignKeyConstraint(
            ["conversation_id"],
            ["private_chat_conversations.conversation_id"],
            name="fk_private_chat_active_conversations_conversation",
            ondelete="CASCADE",
        ),
        sa.PrimaryKeyConstraint(
            "user_id",
            "world_id",
            name="pk_private_chat_active_conversations",
        ),
    )

    op.create_index(
        "ix_private_chat_active_conversations_conversation",
        "private_chat_active_conversations",
        ["conversation_id"],
        unique=False,
    )


def downgrade() -> None:
    """Remove the active conversation pointer."""

    op.drop_index(
        "ix_private_chat_active_conversations_conversation",
        table_name="private_chat_active_conversations",
    )

    op.drop_table("private_chat_active_conversations")

18. MIGRATION ENVIRONMENT FILE
============================================================

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sys
from logging.config import fileConfig

from alembic import context
from dotenv import load_dotenv
from sqlalchemy import engine_from_config, pool

# Load the repository-level .env before accessing DATABASE_URL.
# Repository root:
# C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\.env
load_dotenv(
    os.path.abspath(
        os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "..", ".env")
    )
)

# Make backend/ importable so Alembic can load the application modules.
sys.path.insert(
    0,
    os.path.abspath(
        os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..")
    ),
)

from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import Base, database_url
from app.infrastructure.persistence import beta_access_models
from app.infrastructure.persistence import learning_world_models  # noqa: F401


config = context.config

if config.config_file_name is not None:
    fileConfig(config.config_file_name)

target_metadata = Base.metadata


def get_database_url() -> str:
    """Return the configured database URL."""
    return database_url()


def run_migrations_offline() -> None:
    """Run migrations without creating a database connection."""
    url = get_database_url()

    context.configure(
        url=url,
        target_metadata=target_metadata,
        literal_binds=True,
        dialect_opts={"paramstyle": "named"},
        compare_type=True,
    )

    with context.begin_transaction():
        context.run_migrations()


def run_migrations_online() -> None:
    """Run migrations using a live database connection."""
    configuration = config.get_section(config.config_ini_section) or {}
    configuration["sqlalchemy.url"] = get_database_url()

    connectable = engine_from_config(
        configuration,
        prefix="sqlalchemy.",
        poolclass=pool.NullPool,
    )

    with connectable.connect() as connection:
        context.configure(
            connection=connection,
            target_metadata=target_metadata,
            compare_type=True,
        )

        with context.begin_transaction():
            context.run_migrations()


if context.is_offline_mode():
    run_migrations_offline()
else:
    run_migrations_online()
